HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_08331d0517c136d35d4d","text":"2. **Compact solidity without hollowness activates the anti-birth reading.** Lexical evidence: `ص م د B002` (compact solidity without hollowness), `ص م د B003` (tight bottle stopper/seal), `و ل د B003` (birth/delivery), and `ر ح م B003/B004` (womb; afterbirth distress). Structural cue: `الصمد` stands immediately before the doubled negated birth verbs in 112:3. Assigned roles: compactness/no-hollowness supplies a material image of no internal cavity or reproductive aperture; the stopper variant intensifies sealing/closure; `لم يلد ولم يولد` states the relational denial. Abductive move: I infer a material analogy from `صمد` to the birth negation: the packet supplies solidity/no hollow and denied birth; I supply the image of generative passage being impossible or irrelevant. Reading change: `الصمد` reads as ontological fullness that makes begetting/being begotten incoherent, not just as \"the one asked for.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_18196209983c8dfe949a","text":"1. **The One is the resorted-to center of need, but without reciprocal dependency.** Lexical evidence: `ص م د B001` (intending/resorting to the relied-on one), `ء ل ه B001` (worship and the worshipped one), `ء ل ه B002`, and 112:0 `ر ح م B001` (beneficence toward one shown mercy). Structural cue: `ٱللَّهُ` is repeated from 112:1 and immediately predicated as `ٱلصمد`; the basmalah's mercy precedes this, while 112:3-4 deny filial and peer relations. Assigned roles: `الصمد` supplies the direction of approach and reliance; `الرحمن الرحيم` supplies benevolent reception; the negations block any reading in which the relied-on One needs offspring, parent, or counterpart. Abductive move: I supply the asymmetry: creatures may direct need toward the relied-on One, but the local denials make that direction non-reciprocal. Reading change: 112:2 reads not merely as a title, but as a dependency map: all resort terminates there, while no genealogical or equalizing dependency returns.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_242fe5fc93a88b4c231e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:2:2\",\"root_000882\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kendisine başvurulan üstün kişi benzetmesi anlaşılmayı kolaylaştırır, ancak sonraki soy ve denklik olumsuzlamaları Allah'ı rakipleri ve halefleri bulunan önderler sınıfından çıkarır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2ab577d5d0aee338d8cd","text":"1. **The terminal direction of dependence.** Fixed-ayah `ٱلصَّمَدُ` is directly anchored in `ص م د:B001` (intending and resorting to the relied-on one), while `ٱللَّهُ` carries `ء ل ه:B001` (the worshipped one). The nominal identification places the object of worship and the destination of needs in one subject; 112:4's negated `ك ف ء:B001` removes any equal alternative destination. Worship supplies orientation, resort supplies directed movement, and lack of a counterpart prevents that movement from branching toward a peer. The causal arrow is inferred: the packet does not say that incomparability causes exclusive resort. The ayah now reads as a directional map of dependence, with `ٱلصَّمَدُ` as the endpoint on which need terminates.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3b847eb5a61552a187e1","text":"4. **Enduring support rather than an event in succession.** Fixed-ayah `ص م د:B007` names lasting endurance and continuance. The later clauses are saturated with temporal structures: `و ل د:B003` describes birth events, and `ك و ن:B001` in `لَمْ يَكُنْ` concerns occurrence or presence in time. The enduring branch supplies continuity, while the later negations remove both entry into a birth sequence and the occurrence of an equal. I infer that one who remains the relied-on destination is not positioned as one generated event among successive events. `ٱلصَّمَدُ` thus shifts from a static title to enduring availability that is not produced, replaced, or matched within temporal succession.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_52e34573de3bc77e98dd","text":"3. **Endurance under severity recasts mercy as stable provision rather than soft dependence.** Lexical evidence: `ص م د B007` (lasting endurance and continuance; enduring cold/drought while continuing milk), `ر ح م B001` (tenderness/beneficence), and `ك و ن B001` (being/occurrence across time). Structural cue: the basmalah's mercy comes before, `الصمد` stands as central predicate, and 112:4 negates any occurring counterpart. Assigned roles: `صمد B007` provides continuity under harshness; mercy provides beneficent orientation; `يكن` later supplies the temporal/existential field in which no equal ever occurs. Abductive move: I infer that durable benevolence is locally stronger than affective softness alone: mercy is carried by an enduring, non-contingent support. Reading change: 112:2 allows `الصمد` to color the merciful names as steadfast provision that remains under need and pressure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001","root_000882/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7ee2688ab3f41f2a738a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:2:1\",\"root_000047\",[\"B001\"]],[\"112:2:2\",\"root_000882\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dayanak oluşu, bütün ihtiyaç yönelişlerinin Allah'ta son bulduğu, fakat Allah'ın da bir başkasına yönelen karşı bağımlılığının bulunmadığı tek yönlü bir ilişki kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7fa31fe1571f2e606b2b","text":"112:3's active/passive birth mirror unexpectedly activated fixed `ص م د:B003` as a seal across two opposed channels and sharpened `ص م د:B002` from generic hardness into exploratory underived integrity. Then 112:4's `ك و ن:B001` placed occurrence explicitly in time, making fixed `ص م د:B007` read retrospectively as continuance outside the sequence of generated events and possible peers.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a9d389e330d97e277d5f","text":"4. **The addressed worshipped One is not a chief among chiefs.** Lexical evidence: `ص م د B001` includes a chief/master sought for affairs, `ق و ل B004` includes a titled chief whose word has force, `س م و B007` includes rivalry/boasting, and `ك ف ء B001` later denies equal counterparts. Structural cue: the surah moves from `قل` to `الله الصمد` and then to denials of lineage and peer. Assigned roles: the social \"chief sought in need\" branch gives `الصمد` a recognizable hierarchy; the later denials strip hierarchy of competitive succession, lineage, and matching peers. Abductive move: I infer that the packet first permits a social analogy for intelligibility, then immediately breaks its ordinary social conditions. Reading change: `الصمد` may be read through lordly resort, but not as membership in a ranked political or tribal order.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_be4eccb22b3c84fa6a68","text":"112:3 unexpectedly activated the material branch `ص م د B002`: after reading the birth negations, \"compact solidity without hollowness\" became central rather than decorative. 112:4 then expanded `الصمد B001`: if all resort moves toward the relied-on One and no `كفو` exists, the reliance is structurally one-way, not a mutual exchange between peers. The basmalah also became more relevant in hindsight: `ر ح م B001` allowed `الصمد` to be read as enduring beneficent support, not bare hardness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e288ecf7f08baff206fe","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:2:2\",\"root_000882\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sıkıca kapatılmış olma çağrışımı, sonraki ayette doğumun dışarı ve içeri yönlerinin birlikte kapanmasını önceden haber veren bir mühür gibi işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e4cb8472d88fe5c13af5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:2:2\",\"root_000882\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dayanaklık, değişen koşullarda süren ve doğumla başlayan zamansal olaylar dizisinin parçası olmayan kalıcı bir destek olarak belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e8cea4ace107389912d8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:2:2\",\"root_000882\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dayanak niteliği, bedensel bir sertliği değil, doğum veya türeme yoluyla parçalara ayrılmayan, iç boşluğu bulunmayan bütünlüğü de çağrıştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e9ee0b0b1455631147af","text":"3. **A seal across both lineage directions.** Fixed-ayah `ص م د:B003` contributes the material image of a tightly stopped bottle. Its position directly before `لَمْ يَلِدْ وَلَمْ يُولَدْ` lets the following active/passive pair be modeled as two opposed channels, one outward and one inward. The stopper branch serves as closure; the packet supplies the adjacency and the two negated directions, while I supply the analogy that a seal blocks passage through both. On this reading, `ٱلصَّمَدُ` anticipates the next ayah as a condition sealed against issuing offspring or receiving origin through birth. This is a structural-material activation, not a claim that the title literally denotes a container.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000882/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_eaf9d07cadc73c723e25","text":"2. **Compact integrity outside generative derivation.** The fixed title also activates `ص م د:B002` (compact solidity without hollowness). Immediately afterward, 112:3 repeats `و ل د` in active and passive directions; its `و ل د:B005` extends birth imagery to something generated or derived from something else, and both directions are negated. Solidity supplies the role of internal compactness, while the birth reversal supplies a source-product relation that fails in either direction. The exploratory abductive move is that derivation would introduce an origin/product differentiation that the material image of compactness resists; that implication is not explicitly stated by the packet. `ٱلصَّمَدُ` can therefore read as undivided and underived integrity, not as literal bodily hardness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
